=== [1] ID: 69301317ebf6442f98aa97a1 ===
The function $f$ is defined for all real numbers except for $x=3$.

![Function f is graphed. The x-axis goes from negative 8 to 8. The graph consists of a curve. The curve starts in quadrant 2, moves downward to about (negative 1, negative 5.5), moves upward through an open circle at (3, negative 4) to about (4, negative 3.8), moves downward, and ends in quadrant 4.](web+graphie://cdn.kastatic.org/ka-perseus-graphie/0bdb3a0e4782d0b1bc87f0dc231030d728465636)

**What is a reasonable estimate for $\displaystyle\lim_{x\to 3}f(x)$?**

[[☃ radio 1]]
